Sharon's phone buzzes on her desk.

She reads the first message. Then the second.

She sits back in her chair.

This isn't a supply request. This isn't logistical coordination. This is a strategic partnership proposal with the protagonist explicitly acknowledging the control implications.

She forwards both messages to Fury with a single-line addition: _[Above my authorization level. He's asking to be armed and he knows what that means.]_

Fury's response comes back in under two minutes.

_[Conference room 3. Now. Bring Hill.]_

---

Fifteen minutes later, three people are in the room. Fury standing. Hill reviewing the message thread on a tablet. Sharon present as primary contact.

Hill speaks first. "He's proposing we weaponize him. Intentionally. And he's flagging that we'd be shaping his operational evolution." She looks up. "That's either very smart or very naive."

Fury's eye is on the message. "He used the word 'seeds.' He knows exactly what he's proposing."

Sharon keeps her assessment factual. "He's been thinking about the power mechanics. How generation works. What influences output. This isn't impulsive."

Hill sets the tablet down. "Questions. One: Do we want deeper integration with an asset we still don't fully understand? Two: What happens when he starts generating military-grade ordnance? Three: Who's responsible when that equipment ends up in the field?"

Fury doesn't answer immediately.

Silence for a moment.

Fury makes the call. "Carter. Text him back. Tell him we're interested but this requires evaluation and formal proposal review. Ask him what his current operational priorities are - what capabilities he actually needs, not theoretical artillery. We assess from there."

Sharon pulls out her phone.

She reads it again.

**SESSION SUMMARY:**

Protagonist was granted "The Looting Power" by the Grandmaster via Iso-8 integration. 28 encounters over 21 weeks post-arrangement. S.H.I.E.L.D. partnership established and functional. Background profile obtained. Fury received theoretical power scaling analysis showing Tesseract-comparable trajectory. Protagonist continues established pattern of combat operations and homeless community assistance. Grandmaster predicted Omega-level threat encounter approaching. Two weeks have now passed. An Omega-level threat has manifested in New York.

---

**KNOWLEDGE TRACKING:**

- **Protagonist:** Engaged in Omega-level combat; multiple heroes present; unaware this was predicted by Grandmaster; knows his power mechanics from 28 previous encounters
- **Heroes present:** Captain America, Iron Man, Thor, Spider-Man, Doctor Strange, Black Widow, Hawkeye, others - fighting the threat; may or may not know of protagonist's existence prior to this
- **S.H.I.E.L.D. personnel:** On-site in support/evacuation roles; Fury monitoring
- **Nick Fury:** Watching this encounter with extreme attention given recent power scaling analysis
- **Grandmaster:** Flagged this for detailed observation; predicted it would occur
- **None of the heroes know:** About the looting power's team distribution mechanic or what happens post-combat

---

**THE THREAT - HOUR ZERO**

Lower Manhattan. 4:47 PM.

The dimensional tear opens vertically between buildings. Not a portal. A rip. The space simply splits and something massive forces itself through.

**Zarathos-Nar, Demon Lord of the Crimson Wastes.**

Forty feet tall at the shoulder when fully manifested. Composed of living demonic stone—deep red, near-black, with veins of something that burns without flame. Four arms. Armored naturally in its own substance. No weapons—it doesn't need them.

It came through fighting. Whatever was keeping it contained on its side of the dimensional barrier is still trying to pull it back.

S.H.I.E.L.D. registers the energy signature within seconds. Omega-level classification immediate. All available assets mobilized.

The demon solidifies its manifestation by anchoring to the street. The asphalt cracks in a four-block radius. Buildings nearest the anchor point develop structural fractures.

Doctor Strange arrives first. Begins containment procedures immediately—this thing cannot be allowed to complete full manifestation or the dimensional tear will stabilize.

---

**THE FIGHT - HOURS ONE THROUGH FOUR**

This is not a clean engagement.

Thor arrives within minutes. Brings lightning. The demon's stone hide absorbs it, channels it through the veins, uses it to burn hotter.

Iron Man arrives with War Machine. Repulsor fire at full capacity. Effective but not sufficient. The demon is LARGE. Targeting systems struggle with something that massive and that durable.

Captain America coordinates ground-level evacuation with S.H.I.E.L.D. teams while engaging tactically—identifying weak points, drawing fire away from civilian corridors, calling positioning.

Spider-Man handles building-to-building evacuations. Pulls people out of fracture zones. Webs structural supports that are failing. Fights when he can but his primary value is mobility and civilian extraction.

Black Widow and Hawkeye run tactical support from a S.H.I.E.L.D. mobile command position—targeting assistance, ammunition supply coordination, civilian route management.

Doctor Strange is holding the dimensional tear. It's taking everything he has. He cannot fight the demon AND maintain the seal. If the seal fails, more will come through.

The armored motor-home arrives at hour two.

S.H.I.E.L.D. identifies it immediately. Fury watches the feed.

The protagonist deploys on foot. Full combat loadout. Kree rifle. Doombot-derived systems active. Force fields projecting.

He does not introduce himself. Does not coordinate. Just engages.

Captain America clocks him within thirty seconds. "New combatant, eastern approach, armored, hostile to target."

"S.H.I.E.L.D. asset." Fury's voice comes through the tactical channel. "Designation: Salvage. Enhanced. Friendly. Integrate as available."

Steve doesn't question it. "Salvage, Captain America. Targeting the lower left limb joint. It's showing stress fractures."

The protagonist shifts fire without acknowledgment. Kree energy rifle focused on the joint Captain America marked.

It's effective.

---

**HOUR FOUR - THE TURN**

The demon is weakening. Not quickly. Not easily. But the accumulated damage is mounting.

Doctor Strange has been holding the dimensional seal for four hours. He's past his limit. The Crimson Bands of Cyttorak are fraying.

"I cannot hold this much longer." His voice across the tactical channel is strained. "When it breaks, you will have minutes before reinforcements manifest."

Thor drives Mjolnir into the demon's chest. Cracks spread. "Then we end this NOW."

The combined assault intensifies. Tony coordinates targeting—everyone focuses fire on the chest cavity Thor just fractured.

Spider-Man webs one of the demon's arms, yanks it off-balance.

The protagonist deploys something new—area denial energy web from the AIM drones, week seven. Locks down the demon's lower right arm for ninety seconds.

Ninety seconds of unopposed concentrated fire on the fractured chest.

The demon's structural integrity fails.

It doesn't die quietly. It ROARS. The sound is dimensional—hits on frequencies that don't exist in baseline reality.

Doctor Strange's seal breaks.

The demon collapses. Forty feet of living demonic stone hits the street and the impact registers on seismic monitors in New Jersey.

The dimensional tear starts to widen.

Doctor Strange immediately redirects everything he has left into re-sealing it. "Someone confirm the target is DEAD. I cannot check."

Thor lands on the demon's chest. Drives Mjolnir down. No movement. "It is slain."

Captain America: "Strange, how long do you need?"

"Two minutes to seal. Five to stabilize."

"You have it. Everyone hold position, confirm no secondary threats."

The protagonist walks up to the demon's body.

Fury's voice comes through the S.H.I.E.L.D. channel only: "Carter. He's approaching the body. What's he doing?"

Sharon, watching from the mobile command position: "Unknown."

The protagonist reaches the corpse. Places one armored hand on the demonic stone surface.

---

**THE GENERATION**

The body doesn't dissolve slowly.

It happens FAST.

Forty feet of demonic stone—hundreds of tons of mass—converts in under ten seconds. The matter breaks down into golden light, fragments, streams of energy that separate and MOVE.

Every hero on the field sees equipment materialize in front of them.

Every S.H.I.E.L.D. agent within the operational perimeter sees something appear.

**SPIDER-MAN:**

A compact device—size of a backpack—that materializes on the rooftop beside him. He stares at it.

The casing is demonic stone, polished smooth, with those same burning veins running through it. But the mechanism inside is clearly designed for him specifically.

He picks it up. It's light. Lighter than it should be for solid stone.

The interface activates on contact. Holographic display, simple: **MYSTICAL ANCHOR DEPLOYMENT SYSTEM**

He reads the brief description his suit AI pulls up: Projects tether points into dimensional substrate. Allows web-attachment to空air, buildings, or solid dimensional anchoring regardless of physical surface availability. 200-anchor capacity. Recharges via ambient dimensional energy.

"Uh." He looks at the device. Looks at where the demon used to be. "Did that just—did the demon turn into GEAR?"

---

**IRON MAN:**

The largest single piece.

It materializes fifteen feet from Tony's position. He has to step back.

It's an Iron Man suit. But MASSIVE. Hulkbuster-class, except it's not his design.

The armor plating is forged from the demon's stone. Deep red-black, those burning veins integrated as power conduits. The arc reactor housing in the chest is three times normal size—clearly designed to accept a reactor but built to draw additional power from the demonic material itself.

The HUD activates when Tony approaches. Full diagnostic appears: **DEMON-FORGED HEAVY ASSAULT PLATFORM. MYSTICAL DAMAGE OUTPUT. DIMENSIONAL ANCHORING. INTEGRATES WITH EXISTING ARC TECHNOLOGY.**

"JARVIS, are you seeing this?"

"Yes, sir. The craftsmanship is… extraordinary. And I did not build it."

Tony walks around it slowly. "No. You didn't."

---

**THOR:**

Three items materialize at his feet.

The first: A drinking horn. Carved from demon stone, sized for Asgardian use. The inscription along the rim is in Allspeak: _For the warrior who stands longest._

He picks it up. It's warm. He can feel the enchantment—anything poured into this horn will restore vigor. Not healing. Restoration. Combat endurance.

The second: A whetstone. Demon-forged. For Mjolnir's maintenance—not that Mjolnir typically requires it, but this stone is mystically active. It will enhance edge and impact for any weapon treated with it.

The third: A small chest. Opens to reveal preserved provisions—Asgardian-style. Enough for a warband. High-quality.

Thor looks at them. Looks at where the demon was.

"…Fascinating."

---

**CAPTAIN AMERICA:**

Tactical command suite. Materializes as a compact case.

Steve opens it. Inside: Enhanced communication system with priority override capacity. Strategic analysis tablet with predictive combat modeling—displays probable enemy movement and optimal response positioning in real-time. Team vital monitoring system showing health and status of all allied combatants within operational range.

All of it demon-forged. All of it clearly built for someone running a team in the field.

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed has managed to eat while listening.

"The equipment is stable beyond any reasonable expectation. Fourteen incompatible technological paradigms integrated seamlessly. Seven mystical frameworks operating without interference." He's being precise. "The AI is sophisticated, demonstrates metacognitive awareness, and operates under absolute value constraints aligned with operator principles."

He pauses.

"From purely technical perspective: the capability is reliable, the AI is trustworthy, and the safety parameters are inherently built into the generation mechanism itself."

He picks up a tablet again.

"But I don't UNDERSTAND it. The physics are wrong. The integration shouldn't work. The AI shouldn't have emerged from technological accumulation. Everything about this violates established principles."

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue reaches over and takes the tablet from Reed's hand.

"You don't need to understand it to work with it. We coordinate operationally. You research the mechanisms on your own time." She's firm but not unkind. "Separate the partnership from the research project."

She sets the tablet face-down on the table.

"The question is: do we trust this person for joint operations where our lives depend on coordination?"

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben answers immediately.

"Yeah. I do." He's certain. "Guy stood there for eight hours letting Reed poke his armor and ask his robot questions about consciousness. Didn't complain. Didn't rush us. Just… let it happen."

He gestures with his coffee mug.

"That's someone who actually wants the partnership to work. Not just saying the right things - demonstrating patience and commitment."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed nods slowly.

"Agreed. The behavioral evidence supports trustworthiness assessment." He's analytical even about character evaluation. "He offered unrestricted examination access. Authorized AI participation in cognitive testing. Remained present through exhaustive technical interrogation. Those are trust-building actions."

He pauses.

"And the AI's value architecture is fundamentally aligned with civilian protection and operator ethical constraints. That's reliable partnership foundation."

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue is satisfied with these assessments.

"Then we proceed with operational coordination. I'll reach out to Captain Rogers today about first joint mission." She's planning. "We have dimensional monitoring suggesting Negative Zone instability. Possible incursion within seventy-two hours."

She looks at both of them.

"If that manifests, we request Salvage deployment. Test the framework under real conditions."

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben is thinking practically.

"What's the plan if the loot thing doesn't work like advertised? Or if the stuff we get is useless for our situation?" He's not doubting, just planning. "We going in assuming equipment distribution or treating it as bonus?"

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue has thought about this.

"We plan the mission assuming our standard capabilities. Any equipment generation is additional resource, not primary strategy." She's being cautious. "First operation tests the coordination, not the capability. We verify he integrates well with our team, follows mission parameters, and doesn't create complications."

She looks at Reed.

"The equipment generation is proven from the demon fight. But team coordination isn't. That's what we're actually testing."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed agrees.

"Correct. The power mechanism is reliable based on observed pattern. The operational integration is unknown." He's thinking through scenarios. "Communication protocols, tactical coordination, stress response under combat conditions - all untested with our team specifically."

He's pulled a tablet back out but at least he's eating while reviewing it.

"The AI will help. It demonstrated sophisticated tactical assessment and communication capability. That's beneficial for team coordination."

---

**FOOTSTEPS FROM HALLWAY**

Someone is approaching the kitchen.

---

**JOHNNY STORM - ENTERING**

Johnny walks in, clearly just woke up, hair disheveled.

"Morning. What'd I miss?" He heads straight for the coffee. "Sue said something about new partnership when I got back last night but I was too tired to process."

He pours coffee, turns around.

"Wait, is this about the armored guy who fought the demon? The one Cap brought by?"

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue looks at her brother.

"Yes. Framework approved for joint operations with the Avengers asset designated Salvage. Enhanced individual with post-combat equipment generation capability."

She's giving him the executive summary.

"Reed examined his equipment for eight hours yesterday. Ben assessed character compatibility. I handled operational protocols. We're approved for coordination."

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny sits down with his coffee.

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben laughs.

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

---

There's an emergent AI that achieved consciousness through technological accumulation and doesn't know if it experiences qualia or simulates experience—"

**SUE STORM**

Sue cuts him off.

"Reed. Summary, not lecture." She looks at Johnny. "The equipment is sophisticated. The capability is proven. The partnership is approved. First joint operation projected this week."

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny is nodding.

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben answers.

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny perks up.

"Expert-level gear customized to what we do?" He's interested now. "So I'd get something useful for my specific abilities? Not just generic fire extinguisher or whatever?"

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed nods.

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny grins.

---

**BEN GRIMM**

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny looks impressed.

"That's actually pretty cool. Most people would give the whole 'everyone's special' speech." He thinks about it. "So he's genuine, the gear is good, and Reed got eight hours of science candy. Sounds like a solid partnership."

He looks at Sue.

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue nods.

---

**JOHNNY STORM**

Johnny is processing the scope.

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed is pleased Johnny grasps the strategic value.

"Correct. Resource multiplication across allied organizations. Each victory enhances multiple teams' capabilities rather than single entity." He's back to strategic analysis. "Over time, the compounding effect significantly improves community-wide threat response capacity."

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue is wrapping up the discussion.

"Johnny, you'll meet Salvage when we coordinate first mission. Probably within the week." She's organizing. "Until then, review dimensional combat protocols. If Negative Zone incursion manifests, we'll be responding with guest combatant support."

She looks at everyone.

"Any other concerns about the partnership before I finalize operational coordination?"

**BAXTER BUILDING - GROUND LEVEL ENTRANCE**

**Time:** Early afternoon, 3 days after framework approval **Present:** Sue Storm (waiting at entrance), Reed Richards (emerged from lab when notified of arrival), Ben Grimm (near vehicle bay), Johnny Storm (watching from window above)

**SUE STORM**

Sue is standing at the building entrance when the vehicle approaches.

She's seen surveillance photos. Reviewed technical specifications Reed documented. Read dimensional analysis.

None of it prepared her for the physical presence.

The armored motor-home is MASSIVE up close - forty feet of integrated plating, visible technological and mystical systems, weapon arrays, communications equipment. The demon-stone integration is obvious - dark red-black material threaded through the hull with faint luminescent veins.

It's not trying to be subtle.

Sue activates her comm.

"Reed, Ben - he's here. And the vehicle is… significantly more impressive in person than the technical data suggested."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed exits the building, still carrying a tablet.

He stops when he sees the vehicle.

"Oh." He says it quietly. Then louder: "HERBIE, are you getting sensor readings on this?"

From inside the building, HERBIE's voice: "Affirmative, Dr. Richards. Energy signatures are extraordinary. Multiple spectrums. Significant enhancement since last examination three days ago."

Reed walks closer, already analyzing.

"The demon-stone integration is more extensive than projected. It's not just surface plating - it's woven through the structural matrix." He's pulling up comparative data on his tablet. "The vehicle evolved again. Upgraded itself during the demon encounter."

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben walks out from the vehicle bay area.

He stops, looks at the motor-home.

"…huh." He's processing. "That started as a motorcycle?"

He walks around it slowly - examining from multiple angles.

"This thing's bigger than the Thing-mobile. And it's got WAY more firepower." He's assessing tactically. "Those are energy projection arrays. And those look like missile systems. And what are THOSE?"

He's pointing at mystical ward emplacements that are glowing faintly.

---

**JOHNNY STORM - FROM ABOVE WINDOW**

Johnny's voice comes through Sue's comm.

"Okay that's actually really cool. Can I come down and look at it or are we doing the professional thing where I have to wait for formal introductions?"

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue responds via comm.

"Professional thing, Johnny. Wait for mission briefing." She's firm but amused. "We're coordinating an operation, not conducting vehicle show-and-tell."

She looks at the motor-home.

Salvage is presumably inside. Hasn't exited yet.

She walks closer to the vehicle entrance.

"Salvage, this is Sue Storm. We're ready for mission briefing when you are. Conference room is available or we can brief here at the vehicle - your preference."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed is still circling the vehicle, scanning with handheld equipment.

"The mystical wards are ACTIVE. I'm reading protective enchantment at Sanctum-grade levels." He's fascinated. "And the energy distribution network has expanded since my last examination. New power sources integrated."

He looks at Sue.

"How many encounters has he had since our meeting three days ago?"

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue checks her tablet.

"S.H.I.E.L.D. reports two incidents. AIM cell in New Jersey, Serpent Society weapons deal in Connecticut." She's reading quickly. "Both threats neutralized, no civilian casualties, equipment generation confirmed in both cases."

She looks at Reed.

"Two encounters in three days. The vehicle upgraded from both."

---

**BEN GRIMM**

Ben has completed his circle of the vehicle.

"It's got defensive positioning. Parked for quick exit if needed. Weapons oriented for field coverage. Force field is active - I can feel it humming." He's impressed despite himself. "This is professional operational setup."

He looks at where he assumes Salvage is inside.

"Guy knows what he's doing."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed is trying very hard not to immediately request access to examine the new upgrades.

Sue notices.

"Reed. Mission briefing first. Vehicle examination later IF there's time and IF Salvage permits it." She's setting boundaries preemptively. "We have dimensional incursion to coordinate."

---

**REED RICHARDS**

Reed manages to pull himself away from the vehicle.

"Right. Yes. Mission briefing. Operational priorities." He's forcing himself to focus. "The Negative Zone instability manifested six hours ago. Portal formation probability: eighty-seven percent within next twelve hours."

He pulls up dimensional data on his tablet.

"We need to coordinate containment protocols and combat response before manifestation occurs."

---

**SUE STORM**

Sue is waiting for Salvage to indicate preference - conference room briefing or vehicle-based coordination.

She's being professional, giving him the operational choice.

Behind her, Reed is clearly struggling not to start examining the new demon-stone integration immediately.

Ben is assessing the vehicle's tactical capabilities with obvious approval.

Johnny is still watching from the window above, presumably impatient.

The motor-home is parked in front of the Baxter Building, impossible to miss, broadcasting its presence with active defensive systems and visible advanced technology.

Professional. Operational. Ready for coordination.

But also: Reed is absolutely going to ask about the new upgrades at some point.

It's inevitable.

**THE WAITING MOMENT**

Sue, Reed, and Ben are outside the vehicle.

Waiting for Salvage to exit or indicate briefing preference.

The mission is time-sensitive - dimensional incursion projected within twelve hours.

This is operational coordination, not social visit.

But the Fantastic Four are getting their first in-person look at the mobile fortress that started as a motorcycle and has been evolving through combat for months.

And it's impressive.

Even Ben, who's hard to impress, is clearly approving of the tactical setup.

Reed is vibrating with suppressed scientific curiosity about the demon-stone upgrades.

Sue is managing both of them while coordinating professionally.

All waiting for Salvage's response about briefing location.
